	# IMPERIA COLD WAR — USA PLO initiative BEGIN
	focus = {
		id = COLDWAR_USA_isr_plo_initiative
		icon = peace
		x = 14
		y = 17
		cost = 5
		prerequisite = { focus = USA_X_rogers }
		available = {
			hidden_trigger = { tag = USA IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes }
			country_exists = ISR
			country_exists = PAL
			ISR = { NOT = { has_war_with = PAL } }
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_isr_plo_open_tt
				ISR = { NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ISR_plo_talks_pending } }
			}
		}
		cancel_if_invalid = yes
		search_filters = { FOCUS_FILTER_FOREIGN_POLICY }
		completion_reward = {
			log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: Focus COLDWAR_USA_isr_plo_initiative"
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						PAL = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_PAL_authority }
						ISR = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_ISR_plo_recognition }
					}
				}
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_isr_plo_backing_tt
				add_political_power = 50
				add_stability = 0.01
				ISR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_ISR.197 days = 1 } }
			}
			else = {
				ISR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_ISR.190 days = 1 } }
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_isr_plo_if_accept_tt
				effect_tooltip = {
					add_political_power = 50
					add_stability = 0.01
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 2 }
	}
	# IMPERIA COLD WAR — USA PLO initiative END
}
